# Phase 3 source inventory

Verified 2026-11-22 against local workspace E:/CoNN; repository remote nu-ink/frank_h_torralva-conn, branch initial-repository, HEAD d06568ac87bf370eba627858a12997d88cf30208.

Evidence labels: **V** = verified source/file fact; **S** = static-analysis inference, with limits; **P** = architectural proposal; **U** = unresolved. No project module was imported, no checkpoint loaded, and no training, evaluation, research test, package installation or runtime framework creation was performed. Byte identity and AST similarity are not behavioral-equivalence claims.

Reports: [inventory](PHASE3_SOURCE_INVENTORY.md) | [dependencies](PHASE3_DEPENDENCY_MAP.md) | [compatibility](PHASE3_BEHAVIORAL_COMPATIBILITY.md) | [proposal](PHASE3_PROPOSED_ARCHITECTURE.md) | [risks](PHASE3_RISK_ASSESSMENT.md). Navigation foundation: [registry guide](../../registry/README.md).

## Scope and interpretation

**V:** Recursively enumerated all project-owned Python files, including tests, original run snapshots, source-stage copies, organized packets, reporting repairs and audit/documentation tools. Installed environments, pip/pytest caches, bytecode and Git internals were excluded. No AGENTS.md was discovered in the project-owned tree. Every included Python file was read, SHA-256 hashed and AST-parsed without executing it. Semantic detail is concentrated on the 50 current research modules and the compatibility candidates; the full path inventory below provides coverage of every historical copy without implying a line-by-line scientific certification of each script.

**V:** 708 Python files; 130 distinct byte contents; 91,564 physical lines including repeated copies; 35 package-marker files; 225 files with a top-level main guard. All AST parses passed. Package markers include test packages and historical copies; they are not 35 distinct installable distributions.

| Classification | Files | Interpretation |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| support_tool | 30 | Working scripts, prior audit, protocol review and documentation tools |
| historical_run_tool | 26 | Run-local analysis, verification and repair programs outside named source snapshots |
| historical_snapshot | 294 | Source/original/source_snapshot trees inside results |
| working_module | 50 | Existing research src trees; available does not mean actively maintained or authoritative |
| working_test | 23 | Tests outside historical run/organized copies |
| organization_tool | 1 | Historical organizer; not executed |
| organized_copy | 284 | Copied source/test/report packets; not independently runnable by implication |

Classification is path-based and explicit; it is not a claim that every artifact was frozen contemporaneously. The organizer itself is classified as tooling, not a frozen experiment. The source snapshot and run records in [registry/experiments.yaml](../../registry/experiments.yaml) remain the guide to study identity.

| Boundary | Current source | Modules | Execution surface and scientific scope |

| conn_exp | [conn_experiment_01/src/conn_exp](../../conn_experiment_01/src/conn_exp) | 25 | 25-module shared synthetic/HAR implementation; Experiment 01 runner and 1B/1C/02 orchestration |
| conn_assoc | [CoNN_Associative_Relationships/src/conn_assoc](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/src/conn_assoc) | 13 | 13-module associative Phase 0/1, temporal development and context implementation |
| Phase 3B | [CoNN_Associative_Relationships/phase3b_context_diagnostics/src](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/phase3b_context_diagnostics/src) | 8 | Eight flat modules with bare-name imports; diagnostic wrapper over conn_assoc |
| conn_exp04 | [experiments/experiment_04/src/conn_exp04](../../experiments/experiment_04/src/conn_exp04) | 4 | Four-module fixed-geometry study reusing original conn_exp primitives |

**V:** Two live pyproject distributions exist: [conn_experiment_01/pyproject.toml](../../conn_experiment_01/pyproject.toml) and [CoNN_Associative_Relationships/pyproject.toml](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/pyproject.toml). The declared console command is conn-exp -> conn_exp.runner:main. Phase 3B and Experiment 04 have no separate pyproject at their study roots. A package directory or main guard is an execution surface, not a safe-to-run command.

For example, [run_inspection](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/src/conn_assoc/inspect.py#L33) writes an inspection record, [main](../../experiments/experiment_04/scripts/verify_artifacts.py#L14) writes verification manifests, and [run_preflight](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/phase3b_context_diagnostics/src/preflight.py#L177) invokes prerequisite checks. Their source is cataloged; none was executed.

## Frozen source, historical support and proposals

**V:** Experiments 1B, 1C and 02 retain separate run/source trees. Phase 2/3 has six stage directories: analysis_ready, delivery, phase2_pretraining, phase2_ready, phase3_ready, recovery_io_retry. Phase 3B has a frozen source tree and separate execution-extension code. Experiment 04 has a confirmatory source_snapshot. Organized packets retain historical copies and references. These source sets must remain distinct from present import targets.

**V/S:** [ORGANIZED_EXPERIMENTS/ORGANIZATION_REPORT.md](../../ORGANIZED_EXPERIMENTS/ORGANIZATION_REPORT.md) documents that original Experiment 01 source was reconstructed from a later snapshot and the clean Phase 0/1 boundary is ambiguous. Do not certify those reconstructions as original executing bytes.

**V:** Proposed functionality includes Phase 4 associative memory/retrieval in [CoNN_Associative_Relationships/PROTOCOL_v2.md](../../CoNN_Associative_Relationships/PROTOCOL_v2.md) and Experiment 3D in [AUDIT_20261020/09_Experiment_3D_Proposal/09_EXPERIMENT_3D_PROPOSAL.md](../../AUDIT_20261020/09_Experiment_3D_Proposal/09_EXPERIMENT_3D_PROPOSAL.md). The earlier [docs/research_validation/05_EXPERIMENT_04_PROPOSAL.md](../../docs/research_validation/05_EXPERIMENT_04_PROPOSAL.md) is historical proposal material; an executed Experiment 04 implementation now exists separately. Fixed-width parameterized MLPs and explicit external context appear in the inspected model classes; no implemented associative retrieval store/API, learned coordinates or dynamic neuron merge/retirement lifecycle was found in these trainers. That absence is limited to this workspace, not a claim about all CoNN ideas.

## Complete project-owned Python file inventory

Every file appears once. SHA-256 values are full byte hashes. Identical hashes identify identical source bytes only; runtime roots, imports, environment and artifacts can still differ. ## Limits and next decision

**U:** Which implementation is maintained for future studies, the exact pre-1B executing bytes, clean Phase 0/1 source identity, and per-fit Phase 2/3 stage lineage remain unresolved. Main guards, source presence, static imports and package metadata do not establish independent execution. **P:** Retain this inventory as a dated assessment and approve component-specific contracts before any future extraction. No code extraction or registry change is part of this phase.
